CX-10 /ui/states

CX-02 /capture_fingerprint/baseline_mapping

修訂前（原包保留）
"S1 successful_fingerprint stores RECOVERY-FP hash; CapturedAttempt.asset_manifest_digest hashes compact JSON sorted pair array; captured_fingerprint is immutable attempt fingerprint. M07 fingerprint and M04 recovery baseline are separate projections; formal baseline untouched."

修訂後候選：

"M07 successful fingerprint=RECOVERY-FP-v1; M04 recovery ACK=captured revision+original content semantic profile/digest after original publication proofs. Formal baseline unchanged."
